# Review — 050-constitution

## Summary

050's review of its reopening window, from `734b427c` to `935a91a2`: 13 commits of 050's own, among them `ad4fae5b`, `fcb13a75`, `028e08cc`, `1136b996` and `fb5eaaec`. One agent ran all five passes and read all nine loaded backend and cross rule files whole. It read 15 of the 93 scope entries whole: the constitution, 050's spec, plan and tasks, and 11 scenarios. The other 78 are unread. 36 are generated mirrors (`.claude/commands/ductus/`, `.pi/prompts/`, `.ductus/reference/`), each checked equal to its source after substitution. 29 were read at the window's hunks and the sections they touch. Of the scenarios, `findings-route-by-scope`, `a-canonical-source-is-pointed-at-not-copied` and `knowledge-routes-by-population-not-by-kind` were read at their Behavior, and `a-retired-filename-leaves-a-decision-record` with long lines clipped. `AGENTS.md` was read at the window's four commits' hunks. Nine were not opened. No rule finding. One contract break, fixed in the run: `ad4fae5b` moved the `create-scenario`, `append-task` and `set-status` entries' rules to those primitives' compiled descriptions, so ticked AC2 and AC3 and the Resolved Question that the mirror stays no longer held for them; each is now annotated superseded in part by spec 079, and so is the spec body's mirror paragraph. The class was closed in the plan: every promote-table row dropped without a note (the `create-scenario`, `set-status`, diff-base and durable-contracts rows, by `fcb13a75` and `debcc8cf`) now has one, and the stale "leaving 24 promote rows" count is gone. Three chores fixed: the scenario's retired "markdown-only reference" term, the sync rule's "outside the criteria" qualifier in `AGENTS.md` and the plan, and `AGENTS.md`'s restated reopen sentence, now a pointer. The rule waived two observations with their reasons. Of the stored decisions on files the run read, one stands and is carried; one, on fold.md's step 11 row, no longer fires and expires. The one fix round was re-read clean.

1 fix round(s): 1 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

- other — ad4fae5b removed AGENTS.md's three mirror entries for create-scenario, append-task and set-status, whose rules moved to those primitives' compiled descriptions, so 050's ticked AC2 (canonical text in the constitution or a command reference) and AC3 (each promoted entry's AGENTS.md line points at its canonical section) no longer hold for them, the Resolved Question 'the mirror stays, in every section' is unannotated, and plan.md's Classification header ('23 to promote, 3 already promoted, 28 project-only — 54 total') counts 22 rows in the Promoted universal table after fcb13a75 dropped the create-scenario row with no note, as 072's removals have; only the append-task row gets a note under Third round — `specs/050-constitution/spec.md:210` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — the-constitution-fits-one-read's Behavior still says a one-command rule moves to that command's markdown-only reference, a term ad4fae5b removed from the constitution's matching sentence and from the framework; a stale claim the window left in 050's own scenario — `specs/050-constitution/scenarios/the-constitution-fits-one-read.md:14` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — 028e08cc's mirror of the canonical-record sync rule drops a qualifier: the constitution says a correction is one in spec.md outside the criteria, plan.md or tasks.md, while AGENTS.md and plan.md's W24 row say only from spec.md, plan.md or tasks.md, so a table inside a criterion reads as syncing with no back-edge — `AGENTS.md:82` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — AGENTS.md's Weigh what an option starts entry, as 1136b996 left it, restates the sentence the constitution's §recommendations now carries (a reopen the correct path requires is a cost each option states, never a reason to recommend another) beside the pointer, a second copy of a promoted rule that 050's Promotion mechanism and AGENTS.md's own rule against embedded copies forbid — `AGENTS.md:49` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — principle 3 of §runtime-boundary still says commands assume determinism rather than specifying two executable paths to one result and that a quietly absent requirement leaves both paths alive, wording from the two-paths era that ad4fae5b's message says is gone from framework/ (its grep for two-paths and two paths does not match two executable paths) — `framework/constitution.md:387` — **waived**: the sentence is still true of the commands, which each specify one executable path, and it is the stated reason acquisition halts the run; principle 3's wording is quoted by 021 and 048 and the plan keeps the five runtime principles byte-identical (decided by the rule)
- other — plan.md is 61,679 bytes against the 50,000-byte read size and grew 1,154 bytes in the window (60,525 at 734b427c), so check-artifact-size reports it at 2 read pages, against the succinctness principle f9ea53dd added — `specs/050-constitution/plan.md:1` — **waived**: an advisory read-size finding that /analyze reports and its stored decision settles: the excess is §Classification, AC1's verdict record that the promotion-coverage audit reads (analysis.md's artifact-size and plan-record decisions) (decided by the rule)
